Homotopy - Chapter 216

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a homotopy algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 125. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 507/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 125 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homotopy structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 507. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 633. QED.
